10a3(myang 'das): bsam pa bzhin du ma gyur nas/mya ngan gyis yid gdungs te cang mi smra bar 'khod pa ni/dper na pha ma shin tu byams pa'i bu cig pa/glo bur du nad kyis btab ste/tshe 'phos nas

10a5(myang 'das): tu gzhag nas/phyogs gcig tu cang mi smra bar 'khod do//de'i tshe gzhan yang klung gang+ga gsum gyi bye ma snyed kyi dge bsnyen ma khrims lnga yongasu srung ba/spyod lam dang 
10a6(myang 'das): ldan pa la 'di lta ste/dge bsnyen ma 'tsho ba'i yon tan dang /dge bsnyen ma dpal gyi 'phreng ba dang /dge bsnyen ma phyi ba'i zer sha ka la sogs pas thog mar drangs nas/dge bsnyen ma 
10a7(myang 'das): brgyad khri bzhi stong snyed thams cad kyang /dam pa'i chos yongasu 'dzin pa/sems can tshad med pa brgya stong gdul ba'i phyir/bud med kyi lusu ston cing /khyim gyi chos yongasu smod 
10a8(myang 'das): pa bdag gi lus la sbrul gdug pa bzhi dang 'dra bar rtogs pa/lus la rtag tu srin bu grangs med pa za ba dang /lus 'di la shin tu brtsog pa dang /'dod pas yongasu bcings par 
10b1(myang 'das): rtogs pa/lus 'di la khyi ro dang 'dra bar 'jigasu rung ba dang /lus 'di ni mi gtsang ba ste/bu ga dgu nas rtag tu zag par rtogs pa/lus 'di ni mkhar dang 'dra ste/sha dang lpags pa
10b2(myang 'das): dang /rtsa dang /rgyus pas rnam par dkris pa/lag pa dang rkang pa ni mkhar gyi nyen lta bur gyur pa/mig ni skar khung lta bur gyur pa/mgo bo ni khang pa lta bur gyur te/sems rgyal po
10b3(myang 'das): dang 'dra ba/de'i nang na gnas par rtogs pa/lus 'khar dang 'dra ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis yongasu spangs te/bus pa so so'i skye bo dag ni de la chags par
10b4(myang 'das): byed do//'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug gi srin po ga nas par rtogs pa/lus 'di na snying po med pa ste/'dam bu'i myu gu 'am/e ran 'da' 'am/chu'i chu bur ram/
10b5(myang 'das): chu shing lta bur rtogs pas/lus 'di ni mi rtag pa ste/skad cig ga skad cig tu 'pho zhing/dper na glog gi 'od dam/ga zar po'i chu 'am/sgyu ma 'am/smig sgyu 'am/
10b6(myang 'das): chu la ri mor bris pa lta bur rtogs pa/lus 'di ni 'gyur zhing 'pho ba ste/dper na chu 'gram g.yang sa'i sdong po lta bur rtogs pa/lus 'id ni ring por mi thogs pa ste/spyang ki dang /
10b7(myang 'das): phar ba dang /srin bya dang /'ug pa dang /bya rgod dang /khwa dang /ske ga dang /khyi ltogs pa'i zan du gyur te/'di la mkhas pa shes can su zhig mos shing chags par byed snyam
10b8(myang 'das): du rtogs pa/bi rmig gi nang du rgya mtsho chen po'i chu blugs kyang bzod kyi /lus 'di mi rtag pa dang /mi gtsang ba dang /brtsog pa ni brjod sla ba ma yin par rtogs pa/sa chen po
11a1(myang 'das): @#//'di rgya shug tsam du dril tam/chung ngu na yungs kar gyi 'bru 'am/rdul phra mor gse ba ni sla'i//lus 'di'i nyes pa'i skyon brjod pa ni sla ba ma yin par rtogs pa/
11a2(myang 'das): de bas na mchil ma dang 'dra bar yongasu spangs nas/de'i rgyu de'i rkyen gyis/dge bsnyen ma rnams stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa'i chos la rtag tu nan tan byed pa/
11a3(myang 'das): theg pa chen po'i mdo sde nyan cing 'dzin pa la mos pa/thos nas kyang gzhan la rgya cher ston nus pa/sngon gyi smon lam yongasu 'dzin pa/bud med kyi lus la shin tu smod pa/shin tu 
11a4(myang 'das): skyon che bar shes pa/snying po med par rtogs pa/sems rtag tu rnal 'byor du spyod pa/de bzhin du yang dag par rtogs cing mtha' med par 'khor ba'i rgyun yongasu gcod pa/
11a5(myang 'das): theg pa chen po la skom pas gdungs pa ltar mos pa/bdag shin tu ngoms pas/gzhan yang shin tu ngoms par byed pa/theg pa chen po la rab tu mos pa/theg pa chen 
11a6(myang 'das): po shin tu srung ba/bud med kyi lus su ston kyang yang dag par byang chub sems dpa' sha stag ste/thams cad kyang 'jig rten gyi bsam pa dang /shin tu sbyor ba/ma 
11a7(myang 'das): rgal pa sgrol ba/ma grol ba 'grol ba/dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung rgyun mi 'chad pa 'tshob pa/ma 'ongs pa'i dus su chos kyi 'khor lo skor bar 'gyur ba/rgyan chen pos 
11a8(myang 'das): yongasu brgyan pa/tshul khrims yongasu srung ba/de lta bu'i yon tan tshad med pa dang ldan pa/sems can thams cad la snying rje'i sems bskyed cing /bu gcig dang 'dra bar gnyisu 
11b1(myang 'das): med par mnyam pa sha stago/de dag kyang snga dro'i dus nyi ma thog ma shar pa na/'od kyis reg nas gcig la gcig 'di skad du/deng ni shing sa la drung gnyis kyi drung du 'deng ngo//zhes  
11b2(myang 'das): smraso//dge bsnyen ma rnams kyis mchod pa'i yo byad bshams pa/snga ma bas phyed kyis bzang bar sbyar te/sangs rgyas gang na bar lhags nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la spyi 
11b3(myang 'das): bos phyag 'tshal te/lan brgya stong bskor nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gis/sangs rgyas dang dge 'dun du bcas pa la/mchod
11b4(myang 'das): pa'i yo byad bshams pa 'di /de bzhin gshegsa pas thugs brtse ba'i slad du bzhes shig ces gsol to//de la bcom ldan 'das cang mi gsung bar bzhesu ma 
11b5(myang 'das): gnango//dge bsnyen ma rnams bsams pa bzhin ma gyur pas/mya ngan gyis yid gdungs te/phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//de'i tshe gzhan yang klung gang+ga bzhi'i bye ma snyed kyi li 
11b6(myang 'das): tsa byi'i bu/grong khyer yangs pa can na gnas pa'i skyes pa dang /bud med dang /khye'u dang /bu mo dang /chung ma dang /'khor rnams dang /gzhan yang 'dzam+bu'i gling gi rgyal 
11b7(myang 'das): rigasu gtogs pa/rgyal po'i 'khor dang bcas te thams cad kyang/chos 'dod pa'i phyir/tshul khrims yongasu srung ba/spyod lam dang ldan pa/slob pa gzhan la zhugs shing /
11b8(myang 'das): dam pa'i chos la sun 'byin pa shin tu tshar gcod pa sha stag gcig la gcig 'di skad ces dam 'cha'o//bdag cag gser dang dngul dang /rin po che'i gter mdzod lta 
12a1(myang 'das): @#//bur/yang dag pa'i chos shin tu zab mo bdud rtsi lta bu zad mi shes pa/yun tu 'jig rten na gnas par byas te/bdag cag kyang de dag la rtag tu nan tan spyod par 
12a2(myang 'das): gyur cig/gal te zhal nas gsungs pa'i dam pa'i chos la/dbang za zhing skur pa zhig yod na/bdag cag gis de'i lce gcad do zhes dam bcas shing /gzhan yang khyim nas/
12a3(myang 'das): khyim ma yin par rab tu byung ba dag/tshul khrims las nyams par gyur na/bdag cag gis khyim par phab ste/las byed du gzhugo zhes pa dang /gal te dam pa'i chos la nan tan du 
12a4(myang 'das): byed na/bdag cag gis pha ma dang 'dra bar/rim gro dang /bkur sti bya'o zhes pa dang /gal te dge 'dun las dam pa'i chos la nan tanadu spyod pa zhig yod na/bdag can 
12a5(myang 'das): gis rjesu yi rang zhing de la mthu yang sbyin par bya'o zhes dam bcas pa/rtag tu theg pa chen po'i mdo sde thos par mos shing /thos nas kyang gzhan la rgyas par ston pa de 
12a6(myang 'das): lta bu'i yon tan dang ldan pa sha stag la 'di lta ste/li tsa byi'i bu rnam dag dri ma med pa'i snying po dang /li tsa byi'i bu bag yod pa rnam par dag pa dang /li tsa byi'i bu rtag tu dri med rnam 
12a7(myang 'das): dag yon tan la sogs te/de dag gcig la gcig 'di skad du/kye grogs po dag myur bar de bzhin gshegs pa gang na ba der song ste/mchod pa phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa 
12a8(myang 'das): sna tshogs kyis mchod du 'deng ngo //de nas li tsa byi de dag re res kyang /bal glang brgyad khri bzhi stong dang /rin po che'i shing rta/rta bzhi la bcas pa brgyad khri bzhi stong dang /yid 
12b1(myang 'das): bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che brgyad khri bzhi stong dang /gzhan yang shing tsan dan dang /a ga ru sbams pa brgyad khri bzhi stong snyed bshams te/bal glang de re re'i mdun na rin po che'i rgyal 
12b2(myang 'das): mtshan dang /ba dan dang /gdugs kyang bshamaso//gdugs nang gi chu ngu yang khor khor yug tu dpag tshad gcig khebaso//ba dan nang gi chu ngu yang ring khyad dpag tshad sum cu rtsa gnyiso//
12b3(myang 'das): rin po che'i rgyal mtshan nang gi chu ngu yang mtho khyad dpag tshad brgya pa ste/de lta bu'i mchod pa'i yo byad bshams nas/sangs rgyas gang na bar lhags te/phyin pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi

12b8(myang 'das): dang /tshong dpon theg pa chen po la shin tu mos pas/slob pa gzhan la zhugs pa gang zhig yang dag pa'i chos la smod pa zhig yod na/de dag gi mthus thul pa ni/ji ltar rtsa ba dang /
13a1(myang 'das): @#//shing la ser ba bab pa lta bu sha stag la 'di lta ste/tshong dpon nyi ma'i 'od dang /tshong dpon 'jig rten skyong dang /tshong dpon chos skyong la sogs pas thog mar

29a2(myang 'das): lus/thams cad kyi lus mi rtag pa'i luso//sbyin pa phyis bzhes pa na nyon mongs pa ma mchis pa'i sku/rdo rje lta bu'i chos kyi sku rtag pa'i sku/mtha' yas pa'i sku lags na/
29a3(myang 'das): ci'i slad du sbyin pa rnam pa gnyis kyi 'bras bu khyad par ma mchis shes gsungs sngar sbyin pa blangs pa ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu rdzogs par mi 'gyur ba nas/shes
29a4(myang 'das): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i bar du rdzogs par mi 'gyur te/sha'i mig 'ba' shig thob kyi/sangs rgyas kyi spyan mi thob pa nas/shes rab kyi spyan gyi bar du thob pa ma lagaso//
29a5(myang 'das): sbyin pa phyis bzhes pa ni sbyin pa'i pha rol du phyin pa yongasu rdzogs sa pa nas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa' bar du yongasu rdzogs la/sangs rgyas kyi spyan dang ldan
29a6(myang 'das): pas na/shes rab kyi spyan gyi b.radu ldan pa lags na/ci'i slad du sbyin pa rnam gnyis kyi 'bras bu khyad pa ma mchis shes gsungs/bcom ldan 'das sbyin pa
29a7(myang 'das): sngar blangs pa ni blangs ma thag tu 'tshal nas/de ma thag tu zhu bas tshe dang mdog dang /stobs dang /bde ba dang /so sor yang dag par shes pa thob pa lagaso//sbyin pa phyis
29a8(myang 'das): bzhes pa ni gsol pa dang zhu ba yang ma mchis la/'bras bu rnam pa lnga po yang ma mchis na/ci'i slad du sbyin ba rnam pa gnyis kyi 'bras bu khyad bar ma mchis shes
29b1(myang 'das): gsungs/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa ni bskal pa tshad med mtha' yas grangs med pa'i bar du/zas sam nyon mongs pa 
29b2(myang 'das): dang bcas pa'i sku ma yin/nang gi tha ma'i sku ma yin te/rtag pa'i sku chos kyi sku rdo rje'i sku'o//rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas kyi rang bzhin ma mthong ba ni/nyon mongs pa'i lus 
29b3(myang 'das): zas dang bcas pa'i lus/nang gi tha ma'i laso//byang chub sems dpa' de'i tshe/bsod snyoms bzhes nas/rdo rje lta bu'i tinge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/bsod snyoms 
29b4(myang 'das): zhu ma thag tu sangs rgyas kyi rang bzhin thong nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/de bas na sbyin pa gnyis 
29b5(myang 'das): kyi 'bras bu khyad par med do zhes ngas gsungaso//byang chub sems dpa'i thog ma yang bdud bzhi bcom la/da mya ngan las 'da' khar yang /bdud rnam bzhi bcoM 
29b6(myang 'das): pas/de bas na sbyin pa rnam pa gnyis kyi 'bras bu khyad par medo zhes ngas gsungaso//byangs chub sems dpa' de'i tshe na/mdo sde bcu gnyis rgyas par ma gsungs mod kyi/
29b7(myang 'das): thams cad sngar thugs su chud la/da mya ngan las 'da'i kar sems can rnams la rgyas par phye zhing bstan pas/de bas na sbyin pa rna pa gnyis la khyad par med do//
29b8(myang 'das): rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni bskal pa tshad med grangs med pa'i bar du bsod snyoms mi gsol mod kyi/nyan thos rnams la 'di skad ces gsungs te/sngon de 
30a1(myang 'das): @#//bzhin gshegs pas/gnag rdzi mo nan ta dang /nan ta pa la gnyis 'o ma'i 'jams phul ba bzhes nas/gdod bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs 
30a2(myang 'das): pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyaso//nga ni yang dag par bshos mi gsol te/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor mang po 'di dag gi phyir/khyod kyi mchod pa tham 'di 
30a3(myang 'das): bzhes mod kyi yang dag par na gsol ba medo//de'i tshe na 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor de rnams bcom ldan 'das kyis/tsun da'i mchod pa bzhes pa mthong nas/thams cad dga' 
30a4(myang 'das): mgu rangs te/skad 'thun pas tsun da la legaso//shi na tu legaso zhes smraso//khyod kyi ming btags pa thog tu bab po//tsun da zhes bya ba ni don dam pa la mkhas pa 
30a5(myang 'das): ste|khyod kyis kyang deng 'dir don chen po brtsams pas/de bas na don las ming tsun da zhes btags te/khyod kyis ni tsha 'di nyid grags pa dang /phan pa'i don 
30a6(myang 'das): chen po 'thob cing /smon pa yang yongasu rdzogs par gyur te/ngo mtshar che'o//tsun da mi'i nang du skyes kyang /bla na med pa'i bde ba shin tu rnyed dka' ba thob bo//legaso tsun 
30a7(myang 'das): da|me tog u dum 'ba' ra ltar 'jig rten na shin tu dkon pa ste/sangs rgyas 'jig rten du gshegs pa yang shin tu rnyed dka' la/sangs rgyas dang 'phrad nas dad pa 
30a8(myang 'das): skyed pa yang shin tu dka'/chos thos pa yang shin tu dka'/sangs rgyas yongasu mya ngan las 'das kar mchod pa bshams te phul ba shin tu dka'o//tsun da la phyag 
30b1(myang 'das): 'tshalo||tsun da la phyag 'tshalo//khyod ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu rdzogs te/ston gyi tshes bco lnga'i zla ba ltar rgyas shing yongasu dag la sprin nam na bun gyis mchod na/
30b2(myang 'das): thams cad kyis blta ba dang 'dra bar/khyod kyang de dang 'dra bar bdag cag la sogs pas shin tu blta bar gyur to//khyod kyi mchod pa nang gi tha ma bzhes pas/khyod kyi sbyin 
30b3(myang 'das): pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par gyur te/tsun da khyod la phyag 'tshalo//de bas na khyod ni zla ba yongasu rgyas pa dang 'dra ste/sems can thams cad gang yang mi lta ba 
30b4(myang 'das): med do//tsun da khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//mi'i lus thob kyang sems ni sangs rgyas kyi thugs dang 'dra bas/tsun da khyod ni sangs rgyas kyi sras yang dag pa ste/sgra can 'dzin 
30b5(myang 'das): dang tha mi dad do//de nas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor de rnams kyis tshigasu bcad de/smras pa/mi'i nang du khyod skyes pa//drug po lha las shin tu 'phags/bdag
30b6(myang 'das): cag 'khor rnams thams cad kyis//khyod la phyag 'tshal gsol ba 'debs//mi yi skyes bu khyu mchog ni//yongasu mya ngan 'da' zhes gsungs//bdag cag rnams la 
30b7(myang 'das): snying brtse phyir//sangs rgyas bzhugs par gsol ba thob//yun du 'jig rten 'dir bzhugs shing /tshad med 'khor la phan pa'i phyir//mkhas pa rnams kyi sngags pa yi//
30b8(myang 'das): bla med chos mchog bshad du gsol//khyod kyis gsol ba ma btab na//bdag cag rnams ni mi 'tsho'o//de bas khyod kyis gsol ba thob//kha lo bsgyur ba'i ston pa la//
31a1(myang 'das): @#//de'i tshe tsun da shin tu dga' mgu rangs pa ni/dper na/mi zhig pha ma shi ba las phyis sos pa tsam du tsun da yang shin tu dga' bar gyur nas/sangs rgyas kyi 
31a2(myang 'das): zhabs la byi bos phyag 'tshal te/tshigasu bcad pa 'di skad ces gsol to//legaso bde ba'i don du gyur//mi'i lus kyang legs par thob//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug 
31a3(myang 'das): bstsal//ngan song gsum yang yongasu spangs//legaso bde ba'i don gyur te//rin chen gser gyi ri mthong nas//ston pa kha lo sgyur dang mjal//byol song khamasu skye mi 
31a4(myang 'das): dogs//sangs rgyas u dum 'bar ltar//'phrad pa'i dus kyang shin tu dka'//phrad nas dge ba'i rtsa ba' dang bskyed//yi dwags sdug bsngal yongasu bcom//gzhan 
31a5(myang 'das): yang yongasu bcom pa ni//lha ma yin gyi ris rnams te/khab rtses yungs kar bzed pa bas//sangs rgyas 'byung ba de bas dka'//bdag ni sbyin pa yongs rdzogs 
31a6(myang 'das): te||lha dang mi'i rgyud las thar//'jig rten chos kyis ma gos pa//pad+mo'i chus mi chags bzhin te//srid pa'i bar du legs par spangs//'khor ba'i rgyun las yongasu grol 
31a7(myang 'das): mi'i lus thob shin tu dka'//sangs rgyas 'byung dang 'phrad par dka'//ji ltar rgya mtsho chen po'i nang //ru sbal long ba 'byung ba de//shing gi bu gar mgo chud bzhin//
31a8(myang 'das): sangs rgyas 'byung ba de bsadaka'//bdag gis mchod pa gsol pa 'di//bla med 'bras bu thob par smon//kun nas nyon mongs thams cad ni//snying po med pas thams 
31b1(myang 'das): cad bcom//bdag ni da ltar 'di nyid na//lha dang mi'i lus mi re//ci ste thob par gyur na yang //de la dga' ba mos pa med//bcom ldan bdag gi mchod bzhes pas//shin
31b2(myang 'das): tu dga' ba dpag ma mchis//e ran da'i me tog la//tsan dan bzang po dri 'byung bzhin//bdag ni e ran 'dra ba ste//bcom ldan bdag gi mtshod bzhes pas//tsan dan
31b3(myang 'das): bzang po bsung mngad bzhin//de bas bdag ni shin tu dga'//bdag ni 'bras bu mngon tu thob//bla med mchog gi gnas phyin te//tshangs pa brgya byin lha rnams kyang //lhags nas bdag la
31b4(myang 'das): mchod ba bgyid//'jig rten bcas pa thams cad ni//sdug bsngal chen po'i mya ngan skyes//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das bzhugs pa//yongasu mya ngan 'da' zhes rtogs//tham+sa
31b5(myang 'das): cad 'thun par skad chen pos//'jig rten kha lo sgyur ba med//sems can thams cad mi spang zhing //sras dang 'dra bar gzigasu gsol//bcom ldan dge 'dun
31b6(myang 'das): 'khor rnams la//bla na med pa'i chos ston pa//ji ltar rgya mtsho che yi dbus//rin chen ri rab snang ba bzhin//bcom ldan ye shes yongs gcod pa//ma rig pa'i mun pa ste//ji
31b7(myang 'das): ltar namakha' bar snang la//sprin chod yongasu bsil ba bzhin//bcom ldan yongasu sel pa ni//kun nas nyon mongs thams cad de//ji ltar nyi ma shar nasu//sprin med kun tu snang ba
31b8(myang 'das): bzhin//da ltar sems can 'di dag ni//shin tu ngu bas mig kyang skrangs//thams cad 'khor bar 'khor ba yi//sdug bsngal 'bab klung g.yengs par gyur//de bas sangs rgyas
32a1(myang 'das): @#//bcom ldan 'das//'gro ba'i dad pa skyed pa dang //'khor ba'i sdug bsngal gcad pa'i phyir//'jig rten yun tu bzhugs par gsol//bcom 
32a2(myang 'das): ldan 'das kyis tsun da la bka' stsal pa/de de bzhin no//de bzhin te/khyod kyis smras pa bzhin te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du gshegs pa shin tu 
32a3(myang 'das): dka' ba ni/me tog u dum 'ba' ra dang 'dra ba'o//sangs rgyas dang phrad nas dad pa skye ba yang de bas shin tu dka'o//sangs rgyas yongs su mya ngan las 'da' khar/mchod

32a7(myang 'das): nyid kyang /de'i tshul du rig par gyis shig/de nas tsun da la tshigs su bcad de bka' stsal pa/'jig rten bcas pa thams cad ni//skye ba yod na 'chi ba yod//tshe lo 
32a8(myang 'das): grangs med thub pa yang //tha ma zad par rig par bya//dar ba yod na rgud pa yod//'du ba yod na 'bral bar 'gyur//lang tso de yang mi rtag ste//dar ba'i mdog 
32b1(myang 'das): kyang nad kyis 'phrog/'tshe ba'i tshe yang 'chi bas 'phrog/rtag pa'i chos ni gang yang med//dbang du gyur pa'i rgyal po dag/byin dang mthu stobs zla med kyang //thams cad 'gyur 
32b2(myang 'das): zhing 'pho bas na//'tsho ba'i srog kyang de bzhin no//sdug bsngal 'khor lo mtha' med de//'khor bar 'khor ba rgyun mi 'chad//khams gsum thams cad rtag pa med//
32b3(myang 'das): srid pa thams cad bde ma yin//srid kyi mtshan nyid dngos po ni//thams cad stong pa med pa ste//'jig pa'i chos can 'gyur zhing 'pho//mya ngan skyon dang rtag tu bcas//skyon

32b5(myang 'das): ji ltar dar gyi srin bu bzhin//de la mkhas pa shes rab can//su zhig dga' zhing mos par byed//lus 'di sdug bsngal phung po ste//thams cad mi gtsang

32b8(myang 'das): ni srid pa'i pha rol phyin//sdug bsngal kun las shin tu 'das//de bas da ltar thob pa ni//bla med bde ba 'ba' shig go/de'i rgyu dang rkyen gyis na//spros pa med pa'i 
33a1(myang 'das): @#//mtha' rig ste//bcing ba thams cad yongs bcad nas//deng 'dir yongasu mya ngan 'da'//nga la rga shi na ba med//sku tshe tshad kyang zad mi shes//deng du mya

33b1(myang 'das): pa yun tu 'jig rten na bzhugs shing mya ngan las mi 'da' bar gsolo//bcom ldan 'das dper na/mi bkres pa de ni slar slon mi gda' ste/bcom ldan 'das bdag kyang
